## 🔥 News

🧰 **2026-09 · Open-Source Workbenches section.** Platforms you can actually clone and run now have their own home.

🤗 **2026-09 · Hugging Face Daily Papers links.** Every entry whose paper has a 🤗 Daily Papers page now carries a direct badge, so you can jump straight to the community discussion and upvotes.

🚀 **2026-08 · Repository launch.** First release covering AI Scientist systems, co-scientists, benchmarks, datasets, and scientific environments.

💡 **Ongoing · PRs welcome.** Missing something strong? Open a pull request. One excellent entry beats five weak ones.

## 🤖 End-to-End AI Scientists

Systems that connect several research stages into one loop. Human supervision is still normal, and "autonomous" is not treated as a binary claim.

- ⭐ [OmniScientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558) - Omni-modal, omni-discipline AI scientist that reasons over heterogeneous scientific evidence across modalities. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2608.13558-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Omni-Scientist/OmniScientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Omni-Scientist/OmniScientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2608.13558&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2608.13558) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://omni-scientist.github.io/)
- ⭐ [The AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06292) - Full machine-learning research pipeline spanning ideation, coding, experiments, analysis, writing, and automated review. [![Nature](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature-2026-006633?style=flat-square)](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10265-5) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2408.06292-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/SakanaAI/AI-Scientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/SakanaAI/AI-Scientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2408.06292&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2408.06292) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://sakana.ai/ai-scientist/)
- ⭐ [The AI Scientist-v2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08066) - Drops human-authored templates and explores research directions by agentic tree search with visual feedback. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2504.08066-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/SakanaAI/AI-Scientist-v2?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/SakanaAI/AI-Scientist-v2) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2504.08066&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2504.08066) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://pub.sakana.ai/ai-scientist-v2/paper/paper.pdf)
- ⭐ [Robin](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10652-y) - Multi-agent biological discovery loop connecting hypothesis generation, experimental strategy, data analysis, and follow-up studies. ![Nature](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature-2026-006633?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Future-House/robin?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Future-House/robin) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://www.futurehouse.org/research/demonstrating-end-to-end-scientific-discovery-with-robin-a-multi-agent-system)
- ⭐ [data-to-paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17605) - Auditable pipeline from dataset to paper that traces every quantitative claim back to executable code. [![NEJM AI](https://img.shields.io/badge/NEJM_AI-2025-006633?style=flat-square)](https://doi.org/10.1056/AIoa2400555) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2404.17605-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Technion-Kishony-lab/data-to-paper?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Technion-Kishony-lab/data-to-paper)
- [Agent Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04227) - Human-in-the-loop workflow taking a scientist's idea through literature review, experiments, and a report. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2501.04227-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/SamuelSchmidgall/AgentLaboratory?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/SamuelSchmidgall/AgentLaboratory) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2501.04227&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2501.04227) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://agentlaboratory.github.io/)
- [AutoResearchClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20025) - Multi-agent debate for hypothesis generation and analysis, failure-recovering execution, and experience carried across research cycles. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2605.20025-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2605.20025&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2605.20025)
- [EvoScientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08127) - Evolving multi-agent AI scientist that adapts its pipeline from accumulated interaction history rather than a fixed workflow. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2603.08127-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/EvoScientist/EvoScientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/EvoScientist/EvoScientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2603.08127&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2603.08127) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://EvoScientist.ai/)
- [DeepScientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26603) - Formalizes discovery as Bayesian optimization over a hypothesize-verify-analyze hierarchy with cumulative findings memory, across month-long runs. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2509.26603-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/ResearAI/DeepScientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/ResearAI/DeepScientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2509.26603&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2509.26603) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://deepscientist.cc/)
- [Kosmos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02824) - Runs twelve-hour-plus cycles over a shared world model, coordinating parallel literature-search and data-analysis agents into cited findings. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2511.02824-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/EdisonScientific/kosmos-figures?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/EdisonScientific/kosmos-figures) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2511.02824&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2511.02824) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://edisonscientific.com/)
- [Denario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26887) - Modular multi-agent system that ideates, reviews literature, plans, writes and runs analysis code, and drafts full papers. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2510.26887-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/AstroPilot-AI/Denario?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/AstroPilot-AI/Denario) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2510.26887&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2510.26887) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://denario.readthedocs.io/en/latest/)
- [CycleResearcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00816) - Trains an open research LLM with reinforcement learning from an automated reviewer model, closing a research-and-review loop. ![ICLR 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/ICLR_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2411.00816-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/zhu-minjun/Researcher?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/zhu-minjun/Researcher) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2411.00816&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2411.00816) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://wengsyx.github.io/Researcher/)
- [Dolphin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03916) - Generates ideas, auto-debugs and runs experiments, then feeds analyzed results back into the next idea-generation round. ![ACL 2025 Main](https://img.shields.io/badge/ACL_2025_Main-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2501.03916-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/InternScience/Dolphin?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/InternScience/Dolphin) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2501.03916&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2501.03916) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://alpha-innovator.github.io/Dolphin-project-page)

## 🔬 Co-Scientists & Research Agents

Highly relevant to AI Scientist research, but focused on part of the loop or explicitly keeping the human scientist as decision maker.

- ⭐ [Co-Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18864) - Multi-agent system that generates, debates, ranks, and evolves hypotheses against a scientist's stated goal. - [AstroAgents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23170) - Eight collaborating agents analyze meteorite and soil mass-spectrometry data with literature context to generate astrobiology hypotheses. ![ICLR 2025 Workshop](https://img.shields.io/badge/ICLR_2025_Workshop-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2503.23170-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/amirgroup-codes/AstroAgents?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/amirgroup-codes/AstroAgents) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://astroagents.github.io/)
- [The AI Cosmologist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03424) - Agentic pipeline that plans, codes, executes and interprets cosmological data analyses end to end. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2504.03424-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/adammoss/aicosmologist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/adammoss/aicosmologist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2504.03424&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2504.03424)

- [SciSciGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05559) - Specialist agents for literature search, database querying, analytics and evaluation over science-of-science data. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2504.05559-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Northwestern-CSSI/SciSciGPT?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Northwestern-CSSI/SciSciGPT)

### ⚠️ License traps worth knowing

**AI-Researcher** (`HKUDS/AI-Researcher`, 5.7k stars) ships substantial code but has **no license file at all**, so all rights are reserved.
**Coscientist** (`gomesgroup/coscientist`) carries a **Commons Clause** rider forbidding sale, which is not OSI open source.
**The AI Scientist** and **v2** use a custom AI Scientist Source Code License derived from the Responsible AI license, carrying use restrictions.
**UniScientist** (`UniPat-AI/UniScientist`) has no license file.

## 🔭 Research Stages

Work that targets one stage of the research loop rather than the whole thing.

### 💡 Ideation & hypothesis generation

- [Can LLMs Generate Novel Research Ideas? A Large-Scale Human Study with 100+ NLP Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04109) - Blind study finds LLM ideas rated more novel than expert ideas but weaker on feasibility. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2409.04109-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/NoviScl/AI-Researcher?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/NoviScl/AI-Researcher) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2409.04109&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2409.04109)

- [Paper2Poster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21497) - Distills papers into structured posters through binary-tree layout optimization, evaluated with PaperQuiz reader-comprehension scoring. ![NeurIPS 2025 Datasets and Benchmarks](https://img.shields.io/badge/NeurIPS_2025_D%26B-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.21497-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Paper2Poster/Paper2Poster?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Paper2Poster/Paper2Poster) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2505.21497&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2505.21497) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://paper2poster.github.io/)

- [Agentic deep graph reasoning yields self-organizing knowledge networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13025) - Iterative agentic graph expansion producing scale-free knowledge networks without a predefined ontology. ![J Mater Res 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/J_Mater_Res_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2502.13025-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2502.13025&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2502.13025)
- [Experiences with Model Context Protocol Servers for Science and High Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18489) - Reports building MCP servers exposing HPC facilities and scientific instruments to language model agents. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2508.18489-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2508.18489&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2508.18489)

## 🌍 Domains

Systems built for one science rather than for research in general.

### ⚗️ Chemistry & materials

- [A-Lab / AlabOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13930) - Autonomous solid-state synthesis lab combining ML target selection, robotic powder handling and automated XRD Rietveld phase analysis. ![Nature 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2405.13930-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/CederGroupHub/alabos?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/CederGroupHub/alabos) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://cedergrouphub.github.io/alabos/)
- [GNoME](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-023-06735-9) - Active-learning graph-network pipeline that predicted 2.2M new crystals, 381k stable, expanding known stable inorganic materials roughly tenfold. ![Nature 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/google-deepmind/materials_discovery?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/google-deepmind/materials_discovery) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://deepmind.google/discover/blog/millions-of-new-materials-discovered-with-deep-learning/)
- [MatterGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.03687) - Diffusion model generating inorganic crystal structures conditioned on chemistry, symmetry and target electronic, magnetic or mechanical properties. ![Nature 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2312.03687-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/microsoft/mattergen?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/microsoft/mattergen) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2312.03687&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2312.03687) [![Model](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Model-8B5CF6?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/microsoft/mattergen) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://mattergen.pages.dev)
- [MatterSim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04967) - Universal machine-learned interatomic potential covering the periodic table across 0 to 5000 K and 0 to 1000 GPa. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2405.04967-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/microsoft/mattersim?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/microsoft/mattersim) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2405.04967&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2405.04967)

- [AlphaFlow](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-023-37139-y) - Self-driving fluidic lab using reinforcement learning to discover and optimize multi-step colloidal nanoparticle syntheses. ![Nature Communications 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Commun_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [RoboChem](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.adj1817) - Autonomous flow platform that self-optimizes, intensifies and scales photocatalytic reactions using inline NMR feedback. ![Science 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/Science_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)

- [AlphaProof](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-025-09833-y) - Reinforcement learning system training a Lean prover on millions of auto-formalized problems, reaching IMO silver-medal level. ![Nature 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)

## 🧠 Scientific Foundation Models

Open or landmark models that autonomous discovery systems call as tools.

- [AlphaFold 3](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-024-07487-w) - Diffusion-based joint structure prediction for proteins, nucleic acids, ligands, ions and modified residues. ![Nature 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/google-deepmind/alphafold3?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/google-deepmind/alphafold3) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://alphafoldserver.com/)
- [ESM3](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.ads0018) - 98B multimodal generative protein model reasoning jointly over sequence, structure and function tracks; generated esmGFP. ![Science 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/Science_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Biohub/esm?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Biohub/esm) [![Model](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Model-8B5CF6?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/biohub/esm3-sm-open-v1) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://www.evolutionaryscale.ai/)
- [Evo 2](https://doi.org/10.1101/2025.02.18.638918) - 40B genome language model trained on 9.3T nucleotides across all domains of life at 1M-token context. ![bioRxiv 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/bioRxiv_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/ArcInstitute/evo2?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/ArcInstitute/evo2) [![Model](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Model-8B5CF6?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/arcinstitute/evo2_7b) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://arcinstitute.org/tools/evo)

- [Chai-1](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.10.10.615955) - Multimodal biomolecular structure model matching AlphaFold3-class accuracy without MSAs in single-sequence mode. ![bioRxiv 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/bioRxiv_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/chaidiscovery/chai-lab?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/chaidiscovery/chai-lab) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://www.chaidiscovery.com/)

## 📊 Benchmarks & Evaluation

Evaluation is the bottleneck. These test scientific knowledge, literature grounding, code and experiment execution, whole research workflows, or interaction with scientific environments.

- ⭐ [AstaBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21652) - Broad research suite spanning literature, ideation, coding, and analysis tasks. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2510.21652-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/asta-bench?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/allenai/asta-bench) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2510.21652&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2510.21652)
- ⭐ [ScienceBoard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19897) - Realistic multi-domain research tasks across GUI and CLI environments. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.19897-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/OS-Copilot/ScienceBoard?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/OS-Copilot/ScienceBoard) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2505.19897&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2505.19897) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://qiushisun.github.io/ScienceBoard-Home/)
- ⭐ [ScienceAgentBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05080) - Expert-authored scientific coding tasks grounded in peer-reviewed papers. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2410.05080-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/OSU-NLP-Group/ScienceAgentBench?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/OSU-NLP-Group/ScienceAgentBench) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2410.05080&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2410.05080) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://osu-nlp-group.github.io/ScienceAgentBench/)

- [RE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15114) - 7 open-ended ML research engineering environments scored against 71 human expert attempts under matched time budgets. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2411.15114-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/METR/RE-Bench?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/METR/RE-Bench) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2411.15114&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2411.15114) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://metr.org/blog/2024-11-22-evaluating-r-d-capabilities-of-llms/)

## 🏆 Leaderboards & Live Trackers

Live scoreboards, so you can see what the current ceiling actually is.

- [HAL: Holistic Agent Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11977) - Third-party cost-aware leaderboard hosting nine agent benchmarks, three of them scientific. ![ICLR 2026](https://img.shields.io/badge/ICLR_2026-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2510.11977-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/princeton-pli/hal-harness?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/princeton-pli/hal-harness) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2510.11977&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2510.11977) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://hal.cs.princeton.edu/)

## 📦 Datasets, Corpora & Environments

Corpora, tools, simulated worlds, and domain data that agents feed on. Scoped to scientific-agent use, not an exhaustive AI-for-Science catalog.

### Agent environments and research corpora

- [DiscoveryWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06769) - Interactive simulated discovery tasks in text and 2D environments. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2406.06769-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/discoveryworld?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/allenai/discoveryworld)
- [ScienceWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07540) - Interactive text science environment for embodied reasoning and experimentation. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2203.07540-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/ScienceWorld?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/allenai/ScienceWorld) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2203.07540&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2203.07540)
- [ScholarQABench](https://github.com/AkariAsai/ScholarQABench) - Benchmark and data for scientific question answering over scholarly literature. ![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/AkariAsai/ScholarQABench?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)
- [HypoGen](https://huggingface.co/datasets/UniverseTBD/hypogen-dr1) - Structured scientific hypothesis data released with Sparks of Science. ![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)
- [S2ORC](https://allenai.org/data/s2orc) - Large open scientific paper corpus for scholarly NLP and retrieval. ![Data](https://img.shields.io/badge/Data-2EA44F?style=flat-square)
- [OpenAlex](https://openalex.org/) - Open scholarly graph and API over works, authors, institutions, and concepts. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white) [![Docs](https://img.shields.io/badge/Docs-0EA5E9?style=flat-square)](https://docs.openalex.org/)

### Scientific data and tools

- [Materials Project](https://materialsproject.org/) - Computed materials properties, structures, and discovery infrastructure. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ChEMBL](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chembl/) - Curated bioactivity database for drug-discovery research. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PubChem](https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/) - Open chemical information and compound database. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ProteinGym](https://proteingym.org/) - Protein fitness prediction datasets and evaluation resources. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

### 📄 Scholarly corpora & literature APIs

- [peS2o](https://github.com/allenai/pes2o) - Cleaned 40M-document S2ORC derivative built specifically for language-model pretraining on scientific full text. ![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/pes2o?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717) [![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/allenai/peS2o) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/allenai/peS2o)
- [unarXive 2022](https://doi.org/10.1109/jcdl57899.2023.00020) - All arXiv papers pre-processed into structured full text with a resolved citation network and linked references. ![JCDL 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/JCDL_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/IllDepence/unarXive?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/IllDepence/unarXive) [![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/saier/unarXive_citrec)
- [arXiv bulk data](https://info.arxiv.org/help/bulk_data/index.html) - Official requester-pays S3 bulk access to full-text sources and metadata for the entire arXiv corpus. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CORE](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41597-023-02208-w) - Global aggregator of roughly 300M open-access papers with full-text search and a download API. ![Scientific Data 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Scientific_Data_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://core.ac.uk/)
- [Semantic Scholar Academic Graph API](https://api.semanticscholar.org/) - Programmatic access to 200M+ papers with citation contexts, embeddings, author records and paper recommendations. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PMC Open Access Subset](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/openftlist/) - Bulk full-text XML for millions of openly licensed biomedical articles from PubMed Central. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [bioRxiv and medRxiv API](https://api.biorxiv.org/) - JSON endpoints for preprint metadata, full-text links and published-version tracking across both preprint servers. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [OpenReview API v2](https://docs.openreview.net/) - Programmatic access to submissions, reviews, rebuttals and decisions across major machine-learning venues. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Papers with Code successor](https://huggingface.co/papers) - Papers with Code has shut down; its domain now redirects to Hugging Face Papers, the de facto successor index. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

### ⚗️ Chemistry, materials & molecules

- [Open Reaction Database](https://doi.org/10.1021/jacs.1c09820) - Open schema and repository for organic reaction records including reagents, conditions, workups and measured outcomes. ![JACS 2021](https://img.shields.io/badge/JACS_2021-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/open-reaction-database/ord-data?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/open-reaction-database/ord-data) [![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/open-reaction-database/ord-data) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://open-reaction-database.org/)
- [QM9](https://doi.org/10.1038/sdata.2014.22) - 134k small organic molecules with DFT geometries and 13 quantum properties, the standard molecular-property benchmark. ![Scientific Data 2014](https://img.shields.io/badge/Scientific_Data_2014-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://figshare.com/collections/Quantum_chemistry_structures_and_properties_of_134_kilo_molecules/978904)
- [MD17 / sGDML](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.1603015) - Ab-initio molecular dynamics trajectories with energies and forces for small molecules, used to train force fields. ![Science Advances 2017](https://img.shields.io/badge/Science_Advances_2017-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](http://www.sgdml.org/)
- [SPICE](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41597-022-01882-6) - Roughly 1.1M DFT conformations of drug-like molecules and peptides for training transferable machine-learned potentials. ![Scientific Data 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Scientific_Data_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/openmm/spice-dataset?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/openmm/spice-dataset)
- [OQMD](https://oqmd.org/) - Over one million DFT-computed thermodynamic and structural properties of inorganic compounds with API access. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [AFLOW](https://aflowlib.org/) - Automatic-flow repository of millions of computed materials entries exposed through a REST interface. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NOMAD](https://nomad-lab.eu/nomad-lab/) - FAIR repository and analysis platform for raw and processed computational materials data across simulation codes. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [JARVIS](https://jarvis.nist.gov/) - NIST-hosted integration of DFT, machine-learning and experimental materials datasets plus public leaderboards. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Materials Cloud](https://www.materialscloud.org/) - Open platform for reproducible computational materials science with archived AiiDA provenance graphs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [RRUFF](https://rruff.info/) - Reference Raman, X-ray diffraction and chemistry data for well-characterized mineral specimens. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [QCArchive](https://qcarchive.molssi.org/) - MolSSI public quantum chemistry results archive with a Python client for constructing large QC datasets. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CrystaLLM training data](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-024-54639-7) - Millions of CIF strings used to train an autoregressive language model that generates crystal structures. ![Nature Communications 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Commun_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/lantunes/CrystaLLM?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/lantunes/CrystaLLM)

### 🧬 Biology & biomedicine

- [UniProt](https://www.uniprot.org/) - Curated protein sequence and functional annotation knowledgebase exposed through a documented REST API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [RCSB PDB](https://www.rcsb.org/) - Experimental three-dimensional biomolecular structures with programmatic search and data web APIs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [AlphaFold Protein Structure Database](https://alphafold.ebi.ac.uk/) - Over 200M predicted protein structures with per-residue confidence scores and free bulk download. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NCBI GEO](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/) - Public functional genomics repository of curated expression series, platforms and individual samples. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ENCODE](https://www.encodeproject.org/) - Uniformly processed functional genomics assays across human and mouse, served through a REST API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NCBI SRA](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra) - Archive of raw high-throughput sequencing reads across organisms and study types. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [European Nucleotide Archive](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/ena/browser/home) - EMBL-EBI nucleotide sequence archive mirroring and complementing SRA with its own browser and API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CELLxGENE Discover](https://cellxgene.cziscience.com/) - Standardized single-cell corpus of tens of millions of cells with an API and in-browser exploration. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PRIDE](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/pride/) - Proteomics identifications and mass spectrometry raw data repository hosted at EMBL-EBI. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [protocols.io](https://www.protocols.io/) - Machine-readable wet-lab protocol repository, directly useful as a grounding source for protocol-planning agents. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

### 🔭 Physics, astronomy & earth

- [CERN Open Data](https://opendata.cern.ch/) - Petabytes of LHC collision and simulated data released with virtual machines and runnable analysis examples. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [GWOSC](https://gwosc.org/) - Gravitational-wave strain data and event catalogs from the LIGO, Virgo and KAGRA observatories. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [MAST](https://archive.stsci.edu/) - Space Telescope Science Institute multi-mission archive for Hubble, JWST, TESS and Kepler with programmatic access. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA ADS](https://ui.adsabs.harvard.edu/) - Astrophysics literature and citation database with a full API, the standard discovery layer for astronomy. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [SDSS SkyServer](https://skyserver.sdss.org/) - Sloan Digital Sky Survey imaging and spectra queryable directly through SQL. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA Exoplanet Archive](https://exoplanetarchive.ipac.caltech.edu/) - Curated exoplanet and host-star parameter tables with TAP query access and bulk downloads. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ESGF](https://esgf-node.llnl.gov/) - Federated distribution network for CMIP climate model output and related earth-system simulation data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA Earthdata](https://www.earthdata.nasa.gov/) - Earth-observation data portal spanning NASA distributed active archive centers and their APIs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [The Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00568) - 15 TB collection of 16 diverse physics simulation datasets across fluids, MHD, astrophysics and biology, with unified loaders. ![NeurIPS 2024 Datasets and Benchmarks](https://img.shields.io/badge/NeurIPS_2024_D%26B-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2412.00568-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/PolymathicAI/the_well?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/PolymathicAI/the_well) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2412.00568&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2412.00568) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://polymathic-ai.org/)

### 🏗️ Research-data infrastructure

- [Zenodo](https://zenodo.org/) - CERN-operated DOI-issuing general repository hosting a large share of long-tail scientific artifacts and code snapshots. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [OSF](https://osf.io/) - Open Science Framework project registry and repository covering preregistrations, materials and data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Harvard Dataverse](https://dataverse.harvard.edu/) - Large general-purpose research data repository issuing DOIs, widely used across social and life sciences. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ICPSR](https://www.icpsr.umich.edu/web/pages/) - Primary social-science data archive holding curated survey, administrative and longitudinal study collections. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [GESIS](https://data.gesis.org/) - German social-science data archive and infrastructure for survey and computational social science data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

## 🛡️ Safety, Ethics & Policy

Dual-use risk, research integrity, authorship, and what happens when agents flood peer review.

- [Risks of AI scientists](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-63913-1) - Reference position paper on agentic-science risk, proposing a triadic human-agent-environment safeguarding framework. ![Nature Communications 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Commun_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [Benchmarking large language models on safety risks in scientific laboratories](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42256-025-01152-1) - Laboratory safety benchmark grounded in occupational safety taxonomies, testing hazard recognition by models. ![Nature Machine Intelligence 2026](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Mach_Intell_2026-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/yujunzhou/LabSafety_Bench)
- [SciSafeEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03769) - Safety benchmark spanning chemistry, biology, medicine and physics with molecule and protein representations. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2410.03769-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/KAIMA04/SciSafeEval?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/KAIMA04/SciSafeEval) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2410.03769&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2410.03769)
- [SoSBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21605) - Hazardous-knowledge benchmark across six scientific domains, showing refusal alignment degrades on technical queries. ![ICLR 2026](https://img.shields.io/badge/ICLR_2026-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.21605-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/SOSBench/SOSBenchEval?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/SOSBench/SOSBenchEval) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2505.21605&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2505.21605)
- [SafeScientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23559) - Adds ethical review, prompt monitoring and tool-risk assessment layers to a scientific research agent. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.23559-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/ulab-uiuc/SafeScientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/SafeScientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2505.23559&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2505.23559)
- [Control Risk for Potential Misuse of Artificial Intelligence in Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.06632) - Introduces the SciGuard safeguarding agent and the SciMT-Safety red-teaming benchmark for chemical-science misuse. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2312.06632-B31B1B?style=flat-square)
- [Dual use of artificial-intelligence-powered drug discovery](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42256-022-00465-9) - Landmark demonstration that a de novo molecule generator can be inverted toward toxicity optimization. ![Nature Machine Intelligence 2022](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Mach_Intell_2022-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [Jr. AI Scientist and Its Risk Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04583) - Builds an autonomous researcher and documents concrete risks observed during its unattended operation. ![TMLR 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/TMLR_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2511.04583-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Jr.AI-Scientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Jr.AI-Scientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2511.04583&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2511.04583)
- [Best Practices for Biorisk Evaluations on Open-Weight Bio-Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27629) - Proposes evaluation methodology for biological-risk uplift from open-weight biological foundation models. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2510.27629-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2510.27629&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2510.27629)
- [LLM Novice Uplift on Dual-Use, In Silico Biology Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23329) - Measures whether models give non-expert users meaningful capability uplift on dual-use biology tasks. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2602.23329-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2602.23329&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2602.23329)
- [Forbidden Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06867) - Benchmark separating legitimate scientific queries from dual-use ones to measure over- and under-refusal. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2502.06867-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2502.06867&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2502.06867)
- [Leakage and the Reproducibility Crisis in ML-based Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.07048) - Documents pervasive data leakage across machine-learning-based science fields and provides detection templates. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2207.07048-B31B1B?style=flat-square)
- [REFORMS](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.adk3452) - Consensus reporting checklist for machine-learning-based science, developed across nineteen researchers and multiple disciplines. ![Science Advances 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/Science_Advances_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [arXiv policy update for review articles and position papers in cs](https://blog.arxiv.org/2025/10/31/attention-authors-updated-practice-for-review-articles-and-position-papers-in-arxiv-cs-category/) - arXiv CS now requires prior peer-review acceptance for surveys and position papers, citing LLM-driven volume. ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [NeurIPS 2025 Policy on the Use of Large Language Models](https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2025/LLM) - Authors remain accountable for all content; reviewers may not share submissions with any language model. ![NeurIPS policy 2025](https://img.shields.io/badge/NeurIPS_policy_2025-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [ChatGPT is fun, but not an author](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.adg7879) - Science editorial establishing that AI systems cannot be listed as authors on scientific papers. ![Science 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Science_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [Tools such as ChatGPT threaten transparent science; here are our ground rules for their use](https://doi.org/10.1038/d41586-023-00191-1) - Nature editorial setting disclosure rules for language model use and barring AI authorship. ![Nature 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white)
- [aiXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15126) - Proposes a publishing venue and review workflow purpose-built for AI-generated research output. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2508.15126-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2508.15126&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2508.15126)
- [AI-Generated Figures in Academic Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16159) - Surveys publisher policies on AI-generated figures and proposes practical disclosure and verification guidelines. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2603.16159-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2603.16159&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2603.16159)
- [WithdrarXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03775) - Dataset of arXiv withdrawals with retraction reasons, supporting integrity and reproducibility research. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2412.03775-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/darpa-scify/withdrarxiv?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/darpa-scify/withdrarxiv) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2412.03775&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2412.03775)

## 🎓 Tutorials, Blogs & Talks

Technical reports and announcements from the labs actually building these systems.

### 📰 Blogs & technical reports

- [Google DeepMind, AlphaEvolve](https://deepmind.google/blog/alphaevolve-a-gemini-powered-coding-agent-for-designing-advanced-algorithms/) - Gemini-powered coding agent that discovers new algorithms and improves known bounds. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, AlphaEvolve one year on](https://deepmind.google/blog/alphaevolve-impact/) - Deployed impact across genomics, power grids, and quantum computing. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, Co-Scientist](https://deepmind.google/blog/co-scientist-a-multi-agent-ai-partner-to-accelerate-research/) - Multi-agent research partner, the lab write-up behind the Nature publication. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, AlphaTensor](https://deepmind.google/blog/discovering-novel-algorithms-with-alphatensor/) - Reinforcement learning discovering faster matrix multiplication algorithms. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google, Gemini for Science](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/research/gemini-for-science-io-2026/) - Hypothesis generation, computational discovery, literature insights, and science skills. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Google Research, AI co-scientist](https://research.google/blog/accelerating-scientific-breakthroughs-with-an-ai-co-scientist/) - Official overview of the multi-agent hypothesis engine. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind and Isomorphic Labs, AlphaFold 3](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/products/google-deepmind-isomorphic-alphafold-3-ai-model/) - Structure prediction extended to proteins, nucleic acids, ligands, and ions. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, Claude Science](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-science-ai-workbench) - An AI workbench for scientists, the closed reference point for open workbench efforts. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, Claude for Life Sciences](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-for-life-sciences) - Connectors for Benchling, PubMed, and 10x, plus science-specific agent skills. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, AI for Science Program](https://www.anthropic.com/news/ai-for-science-program) - Free API credits for researchers running scientific workloads. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Meta FAIR, OMol25 and fairchem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08762) - Open molecular dataset, models and codebase for chemistry and materials discovery. ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.08762-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/facebookresearch/fairchem?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/facebookresearch/fairchem)
- [Microsoft, Discovery](https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/transforming-rd-with-agentic-ai-introducing-microsoft-discovery/) - Agentic R&D platform, used to screen a non-PFAS datacenter coolant. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Microsoft-Blog-0078D4?style=flat-square)
- [Microsoft Research, Aurora](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/blog/introducing-aurora-the-first-large-scale-foundation-model-of-the-atmosphere/) - First large-scale foundation model of the atmosphere. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Microsoft-Blog-0078D4?style=flat-square)

- [How to build an AI scientist, first peer-reviewed paper spills the secrets](https://doi.org/10.1038/d41586-026-00899-w) - Nature's write-up of what the first peer-reviewed AI Scientist paper actually reveals. ![Nature](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nature-2026-006633?style=flat-square)
- [The AI co-scientist is here](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41591-026-04275-z) - Nature Medicine on what a hypothesis-generating co-scientist changes for biomedical labs. ![Nat Med](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Med-2026-006633?style=flat-square)

## Contributing

Focused additions, corrections, and better primary links are all welcome. Read [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md) first.

A strong entry has at least one of these.

- a peer-reviewed paper or a technically substantive preprint;
- a public implementation, benchmark, dataset, environment, or reproducible artifact;
- an evaluation measuring scientific validity, reproducibility, tool use, or research productivity;
- a clear tie to literature review, ideation, experiment design, experimentation, analysis, or writing.

Prefer one excellent entry over several weak ones. Generic chat wrappers, unsupported "AI Scientist" marketing, and papers with no scientific-agent relevance are out of scope.
